# Unsure

Moved verbatim from HANDOFF.md. Triage plan: [HANDOFF.md](../HANDOFF.md) (Unsure plan).

Compiler: the shapes were tried under 10.0 LA unless the entry or its heading names 10.0 GA (the reference compiler since 2026-10-22). `tools/permute.py` may retry LA shapes under GA (its skip rows in docs/unsure_tried.csv, from `tools/unsure_rows.py`, are per compiler); a hand retry of one needs a new reason. New entries say "under 10.0 GA" or go under a heading that does.

### Unsure

Left unnamed or capped. Do not retry the listed C shapes.

On 2026-10-21 each got a first pass from Ghidra (tools/ghidra_decomp.py), in its own file; the shapes tried and what is left are in each file's DRAFT comment (compiled vs EXE bytes, permute.py differing): PlayLeagueGame 4,095 / 4,095 (28), TradeScreenLoop 3,940 / 3,943 (265), LeadersScreen 3,561 / 3,782, SportsDesk 3,422 / 4,567 (720, code still missing), GameLineEditorLoop 5,243 / 5,242 (384), DbLineEditorLoop 5,673 / 5,678 (376), LeagueTeamSelect 5,882 / 5,888 (339), CalendarScreen 6,036 / 6,040 (505), GameSummaryScreen 7,755 / 8,023 (802; when marked, its block starts at 2D33E: gsuminitff, the initializer of its local seasonfh[2], sits before its jump table as in the EXE), SimulateGame 9,902 / 9,868 (721). The notes from reading the asm before them:
  * TradeScreenLoop (3EF89, 3,943 bytes, segment 032; self-contained): the menu engine of RunMenu (RunMenu.c) with the trade screen added (DrawTradeScreen, DrawTradeRow, the jersey of the player under the cursor via MakeJerseyShape / sub_B4DD4). Four register and four stack parameters; ebp frame biased by -72h (as PlayoffTreeMenu / EditRosters), frame struct pos[4][2], bg[4], sel[4], nitems[4], menus[4], ..., item 26h, level 2Ah, my 2Eh, mx 32h, oldmy 36h, oldmx 3Ah, i 42h, the pointer art 46h, the three colours 52h-5Ah, the pointer block 5Eh, depth 6Eh (offsets from the biased ebp); the hand pointer hot spot is 1Ch / 20h.
  * PlayLeagueGame (36B93, 4,095 bytes, segment 028): plays one league game and writes the results (FileOpenRW / FileWriteAt, strcpy of the file names, SetDialogColors); the parameter arrives in eax and is kept in edi.
  * SportsDesk (1935D, 4,567 bytes, segment 010): the front-end desk loop (pointer, FadePalStep, SetScreenTitle, TicksSinceLastCall).
  * GameLineEditorLoop (76AF5, 5,242 bytes) and DbLineEditorLoop (73A18, 5,678 bytes), segment 054: twins (the in-game and the database line editor): ebp frames biased by -76h / -7Ah, a char parameter in bl homed at ebp+72h, the menu engine (MenuHitTest, DrawMenuItemSel) plus jerseys (MakeJerseyShape, sub_931FC). Draft one and derive the other.
  * LeagueTeamSelect (38B4F, 5,888 bytes, segment 029): the league setup team grid (HighlightGridCell, RestoreGridCellBg); four register parameters, the first kept in esi.
  * CalendarScreen (34821, 6,039 bytes, segment 027): the season calendar (DrawCalendarDay, DrawMenuBar); three register parameters (eax homed, edx in edi, ebx in ebp).
  * GameSummaryScreen (2D35A, 8,023 bytes, segment 022; one jump table, cases p1, p2, p3, ot, finalot, final, period): the game summary pages (DrawGadgetByType, sub_913D0, ReadKeyRec as in TonightsGameScreen); two char parameters in dl / bl homed in the frame.
  * SimulateGame (452C5, 9,868 bytes, segment 035): the game simulation without graphics (RandMod, SimAddPair, rand_, ReadTeamRec / WriteTeamRec, KeyDbPtr, SeasonDbPtr); no screen code, so the most mechanical of the ten to transcribe.
